# I-RULE01: Seedream compact prompts (one prompt per frame, text only)

Each prompt is about 1,500 to 2,200 characters, with the locked world first.

### PT-001 (1792 characters)

Cinematic 16:9 widescreen storyboard frame, photoreal. Epic wide establishing shot at dawn, the fixed hero view from the south and slightly above, slow drift. The whole of Igara before the break at dawn, from the hero view, the island filling the middle of the picture. The Throne stands on the mountain with the six bars of fire above it. Skycraft glide in and out along the skyport trailing thin amber light, and a very few tiny people walk the skyport deck behind its rail. A living, organic, bronze-green Dragon glides high in the sky at the upper left with its wings spread and no fire on it. A huge pale ringed planet and two small moons fill the upper right. The low gold sun rises at the far right horizon, backlighting the clouds with long soft rays; floating amber lanterns and flocks of birds fill the air, and mist from the waterfalls rises back up through the clouds. Beautiful, peaceful, vast. Still from an Oscar-winning Hollywood live-action film, large-format cinema camera, photographic realism, real skin and fabric, lighting and palette as described in the scene. People are fair, European-looking, bare-headed, calm faces, in premium tailored stone-grey, silver-grey, beige or taupe clothes (long open coat-robe over fitted tunic, fitted high-necked bodysuit with ankle boots, or long high-collared gown), no two alike, never poor clothes; at most 6 people, children 2 or 3 at most. Spaceships are manta-ray shaped skycraft, organic, never round. ### PT-002 (2615 characters)

Cinematic 16:9 widescreen storyboard frame, photoreal. Wide shot from the same fixed hero view as the first frame, tilted up to show the sky. The same island from the same hero view as the first frame, now framed to show the sky: the island fills the lower third of the picture with the Throne on its green mountain at the centre, exactly six horizontal bars of fire stacked directly above the Throne, and above them the huge Eye, woven from the same gold energy as the six bars, filling the upper sky, its iris turned down toward the island and the Throne, awake and watching over the world. Below, the rock-carved homes glow with lights in their oval windows and skycraft glide from the skyport. Peaceful, majestic, a presence watching over everyone. Characters and places: THE EYE: a huge almond-shaped eye formed out of the sky itself, not a physical eye: made entirely of soft glowing energy and light-haze, translucent and blended into the clouds so it reads as part of the atmosphere. About as wide as the whole island. The almond outline is only a faint pale cloud-light halo that merges into the clouds; in the middle sits a bright round iris of warm amber-gold light made of fine radial strands like sun rays, and at its centre a narrow vertical diamond-shaped pupil, deep brown-black like a window into deeper sky. The iris is turned downward toward the island and the Throne: it is awake, attentive and watching over the people, the vast presence of a great unseen being of which we see only this eye, calm and kind, never angry. Faint thin filaments of light fade away into the clouds. Clouds drift in front of and behind it so it never looks like a sticker. No eyelids, no lashes, no skin, no veins, no wet surface, no flames, no lightning, no sparks. Only the eye, no face, no body. ### PT-016 (2531 characters)

Cinematic 16:9 widescreen storyboard frame, photoreal. Intimate medium close shot, shallow depth of field, warm lamplight. Inside the family home carved in rock, with a round window showing the island in soft morning light. The mother sits on a low stone seat and holds a tiny wild ember in her cupped palm; it jumps and flickers. The sky is a flat, pale, dead grey with no gold light and no sun glare; the air is still and silent. Above the Throne hang exactly six straight horizontal bars, equal length, evenly spaced like a ladder, completely dark and unlit, dull grey like cold iron, with no glow. The green island below looks strangely still, fields empty, no people on the terraces, only a few lights in the rock-carved windows. At the far right end, on the long S-curved skyport deck, a second even smaller figure, the boy in a grey-beige tunic, runs along the deck toward the foot of the staircase. ### PT-041 (2230 characters)

Cinematic 16:9 widescreen storyboard frame, photoreal. Huge wide shot of the Eye. The vast Eye of golden sky-energy above the world closes slowly, its almond outline narrowing to a thin line of light over the amber iris. As it shuts, a ring of fire flares along the outline and dies into thin smoke, the first fire the Eye has ever shown. The sky is left dark. Awe and sorrow. Characters and places: THE EYE: a huge almond-shaped eye formed out of the sky itself, not a physical eye: made entirely of soft glowing energy and light-haze, translucent and blended into the clouds so it reads as part of the atmosphere. About as wide as the whole island. The almond outline is only a faint pale cloud-light halo that merges into the clouds; in the middle sits a bright round iris of warm amber-gold light made of fine radial strands like sun rays, and at its centre a narrow vertical diamond-shaped pupil, deep brown-black like a window into deeper sky. The iris is turned downward toward the island and the Throne: it is awake, attentive and watching over the people, the vast presence of a great unseen being of which we see only this eye, calm and kind, never angry. Faint thin filaments of light fade away into the clouds. Clouds drift in front of and behind it so it never looks like a sticker. No eyelids, no lashes, no skin, no veins, no wet surface, no flames, no lightning, no sparks. Only the eye, no face, no body. ### PT-060 (6355 characters)

Cinematic 16:9 widescreen storyboard frame, photoreal. ELIAS IS COMPLETELY ALONE: he is the only person in the whole picture. No other human anywhere: no family, no villagers, no crowd, no distant figures, no silhouettes, nobody at the door or on any island. Extreme wide view from just behind and above the Starting Shelf in the south-west, looking north-east up the whole climb. Island positions, sizes, spacing and heights as described, in the exact look of the game's key art: warm gold-amber cinematic dusk, dark bronze-lit stone, a sea of clouds, photoreal. THE FEELING: this is a world far too big and too complex to finish in an hour, and the goal is not visible yet. It is a SHATTERED WORLD, not a path: a whole planet was blown apart and the ten numbered islands are only the biggest surviving pieces. Fill the frame with wreckage at every depth: torn rock shards and slabs in the foreground, colossal broken crust plates hanging far behind, a few shards cracked with molten amber. The numbered islands stay the largest and best lit so a first climb can be read, but the route is long, indirect and mostly unseen. THE GOAL IS HIDDEN: the Throne spire at the top left is almost lost in layers of haze and distant shards, only a faint thin dark spire with a tiny amber glint, so the player cannot yet tell where the climb ends. Every island carries tall dark ruined bronze-lit towers and spires, as in the references: tapering tower ruins with sheared tops on the plateau (5), capped drum towers and one needle spire on the arena (7), twin towers with a broken arch on the canyon (8), a gatehouse tower on the summit stair, with glowing amber lanterns on the tallest. Where there is a gap between islands, the crossing is a RUNG BRIDGE: two chains with glowing amber horizontal bars as rungs, exactly like the glowing bars of the six lines, as in The Leap reference; some gaps have no bridge at all. Not a plank-and-rope bridge. In the foreground, step 1, the dark cracked Starting Shelf with its bronze ring disc and two lantern posts, very large in the lower frame, and the tiny Elias with the Cinder at its edge looking up. Clearly BELOW the Eye and never hidden by it, the six sky lines hang large and separate as six long glowing amber bars, each one wider than the biggest island, as in the Summit reference. The RING OF FIRE: a vast thin ring of dormant fire encircles the whole world far away on the horizon, a line of smouldering embers and low dark flames curving around everything, so its enormous size shows. It is not yet ignited, it only smoulders, and it is the proof of how large the world is. The Fire Dragon is MASSIVE and unmissable, as in the Flying reference: a huge winged dragon with dark bronze-black scales, wings spread wide and veined with molten amber, banking in side view across the upper middle of the frame, trailing sparks. Two LARGE planets fill the sky, as in the references: a huge ringed planet and a huge cracked glowing ember planet, both partly hazed. Warm low sun, long rim light on the towers, layered haze so each island is hazier than the last. Only the labels listed below. Soft gold-amber haze, warm light on the high spire, the lowest tiers in cooler shadow. Characters and places: the world is NOT flat: it is a six-tier climb of broken floating islands at rising heights. The Starting Shelf is the lowest island at the lower left. Beside it a small twisted stepping island and a medium island with a windbreak ridge. Then a field of drifting moving platforms rising to a large plateau, a circular arena to the east, a canyon cliff pair far to the north-east, a drifting cluster of fragments across the north, and the highest point of all, a slender summit spire crowned by the Throne at the upper left. Every tier sits higher than the last, with cloud seas below and between them. The distances are vast: the Throne is about 700 m from the start, roughly seventeen widths of the Starting Shelf, and about 360 m higher, so from island 1 it is a tiny spire at the far horizon with ten islands and wide gaps of cloud and void between; the gaps are as wide as or wider than the islands, the bridges are long thin threads and some gaps have no bridge at all, only drifting slabs; the Starting Shelf Island: a ragged floating shelf of dark rock, an irregular cracked spawn terrace with the sunken bronze arrival disc where the player appears, a broken worn road to a crooked weathered bronze beam lying across a jagged meandering crack, a broken forecourt and three worn steps to the locked door cut into a huge black cliff with one enormous deep mountain of boulder rock (36 m shoulder, 60 m summit, two high peaks of the same mass) of the same dark rock growing out of it, no masonry, thick rusted chains hanging from bronze rim rings, bare dead trees and dry grass. Elias's expression stays calm, composed and restrained, at most a very faint soft look, never a broad smile or grin. WORLD RULES: this is a broken sky-world of dark floating rock islands above a sea of cloud; there is no ocean, no sea horizon, no beach, no water, no Greek or classical columns, no gothic or Victorian buildings; any ruins are tall dark bronze-lit tapering towers carved from the same dark rock. The Cinder is always a head-sized floating faceted polyhedron with a gold-bronze frame, glass-like facets and a glowing ember core, never a rock, never a spaceship. The six sky lines are exactly six straight horizontal bars of equal length stacked above one another like a ladder, never vertical, never curved, never comet streaks; before the awakening they are dark and unlit. The Eye in the sky is calm and dormant: an almond-shaped eye woven from thin pale-gold energy threads in the cloud, a soft cloud-edged outline, a gold filament iris, a translucent dusk-blue diamond pupil; no flames, no lightning, no sparks, not red, not black, not a burning eye. Still from an Oscar-winning Hollywood live-action film, large-format cinema camera, photographic realism, real skin and fabric, lighting and palette as described in the scene. ### PT-220 (2547 characters)

Cinematic 16:9 widescreen storyboard frame, photoreal. Final wide frame. A last wide view from the Throne summit, the highest point, looking down the stepped tiers of the quiet awakened world at dusk: steady lantern flames across the broken floating islands, six burning bars in the sky, the Eye closed, the dragon resting, the tiny seated figure of Elias. Calm completion, no explosion. Gentle warm gold light fading to dusk. Far out around the whole world the gigantic ring of fire still burns steadily in the sky. Far out around the whole world the gigantic ring of fire still burns steadily in the sky. Far out around the whole world the gigantic ring of fire still burns steadily in the sky. Characters and places: the world is NOT flat: it is a six-tier climb of broken floating islands at rising heights. The Starting Shelf is the lowest island at the lower left. Beside it a small twisted stepping island and a medium island with a windbreak ridge. Then a field of drifting moving platforms rising to a large plateau, a circular arena to the east, a canyon cliff pair far to the north-east, a drifting cluster of fragments across the north, and the highest point of all, a slender summit spire crowned by the Throne at the upper left. Every tier sits higher than the last, with cloud seas below and between them. The distances are vast: the Throne is about 700 m from the start, roughly seventeen widths of the Starting Shelf, and about 360 m higher, so from island 1 it is a tiny spire at the far horizon with ten islands and wide gaps of cloud and void between; the gaps are as wide as or wider than the islands, the bridges are long thin threads and some gaps have no bridge at all, only drifting slabs.
